ParlaMint-ES-GA_2017-12-04-DSPG051.u331	Thank you. Question by Da Eva Solla Fernández and two other Members of the G.P. of En Marea, on the measures to be taken by the Government of Greece to ensure the right to health of the neighbours affected by lindane pollution in the municipality of Porriño
ParlaMint-ES-GA_2017-12-04-DSPG051.u332	You have the floor to Mrs. Solla Fernández.
ParlaMint-ES-GA_2017-12-04-DSPG051.u333	Thank you, President. Counselor, good afternoon. I'm glad you're here today because we're talking about a serious matter. We're going to talk about a subject of extreme urgency and gravity: the impact of the health of the neighbors and neighbors of the Puppies because of the Lindano. Between 1947 and 1964, Zeltia made Lindano and other toxics in her Porriño factory. The Lindan, as his lords know, is a chemical that was used as a pesticides, known in Galicia, the most common, such as ZZ. This product is a lipophilic product, that is to say, it is fixed to fat and it is deposited in it without it being eliminated. Absorb both by dermal means, through the skin, as inhaled, or by injection, by means of water or by consuming vegetable or animal food containing it. It has a high bioaccumulation and is qualified as a persistent organic compound. In 1979 the European Economic Community had already banned its agricultural use, in 1984 it regulated the waste and in 2008 any use was prohibited for its extreme toxicity. Directive 2013/39/EU of the European Parliament also lists the catalogue as a priority and dangerous substance. The World Health Organization has catalogued Lindane, the content of scientific evidence, as cancer for humans, within Group 1 where the most dangerous contaminants are grouped. It causes tumors, liver damage, hormonal disorders and other problems, and, as we said, it doesn't get rid of the body. In the municipality of Porriño it is estimated that there could be 1,000 tons of waste from the production derived from the lindane; in many cases, in the place where the factory was previously located, and on which official shelters were built in 1975. Since then, no administration has taken any effective action. In 1999, Junta launched an analysis, a study of the soil, and the only thing that was made effective, practically, were the analyses themselves, measurements of the concentrations of lindane isomers. Mr. Almuña, there is a public health problem of the first order and we are alarmed that you are not currently treating this as the first health problem facing the Council of Galicia at the moment. Before the appearance, again, of lindane outdoors, the barbarity of asphalt was allowed above, they told the neighbors not to drink and not shower, and it took you three days to close some well. They're not paying attention to the health of neighbors and neighbors. The Lindan still appears buried in the streets, and there are houses of official protection and even a park on top. This photograph is a few days ago and continues to appear pretty on the floor. In Aragon they are beginning a encapsulation process and are attending this situation with individual protective equipment. ParlaMint-ES-GA_2017-12-04-DSPG051.u337	Mr. President, good afternoon again. Thank you. The first thing I have to say is that soil pollution by lindane from the Porriño Town Hall is a problem that had its origin several decades ago, from the residue that was generated in the process of producing the saying insecticide by the companies that were implanted in the industrial district of Torneiros. Currently, the insecticide saying has been banned since 2001, so it is not manufactured, but Galicia stopped manufacturing it many years before this prohibition; specifically, in 1964. These wastes, given their mechanical characteristics, have been used for years as a firm and filled with various ways of the municipality of Porriño. While there was good belief that the scope of the problem was confined to the area of the Torneiros polygon, as a result of the follow-up by the Mino-Sil Hydrographic Confederation, there was the possibility that this contaminant would be present in the soils in a new area called Source-The Contrast. In view of these indications, the Galician Union commissioned the study of the company Applus+ to try to determine the source of the contamination in this area, through the Environmental Advice. Even if we were based on the hypothesis of possible soil contamination, we had no information on its location. In the last month of October, during the execution of works of the sewer network on a street in the Fonte-O Contrasto area, the presence of a large amount of Lindane was detected, which, according to the neighbors, was used several decades ago in the filling and firm of the road. From there we work in coordination with the Environmental and Health Counsellors, along with the Council of the Child and also the Hydrographic Confederation. In addition, we held meetings involving public health and primary care personnel and managers of Vigo's EOXI, with the aim of providing information on this matter to professionals, so that possible victims could be identified and provided adequate attention. The analytical results confirm that the white residue is lindane and the soil sample results from the presence of this toxic. At first, through the Porriño Town Hall, the recommendation is made that people in the affected area do not consume water from nearby wells, sources or sources if analytical results are not available indicating the absence of lindane contamination. So, the first thing you say, what actions? The first, of course, is to eliminate the possibility of this toxic to human consumption. In addition, taking into account the results of the soil samples, while the characterization and delimitation of the affected area are not carried out, also through the Porriño Town Hall, it was recommended to the neighbors that no grown food be consumed in the affected area. In parallel to all of this, and in a coordinated manner between the Health Council and the Environment Council, and, as I say, the Porriño Town Hall, a field work was started in order to carry out the sampling of the wells and water flows that use the neighborhoods of the affected area, in order to adjust the recommendations given so far and make the use of some of these waters compatible with the assurance that there is no exposure to water contaminated by Lindano. 60 water tests have been performed today, and in 2/3 the result is that in 2/3 there is no contamination...
